In an early December 2023 meeting with Netanyahu and other members of the war cabinet, family members of those who are still held hostage by Hamas forces showed their outrage and frustration with the continued situation. In a published recording, Netanyahu can be heard blaming the end of the November 2023 cease-fire on Hamas while family members and supporters can be heard calling him a liar. Some former hostages also attended the meeting and spoke about the conditions they were held in, which were exasperated by Israeli airstrikes.[^cite_note-332] One unnamed freed hostage from the kibbutz [Nir Oz](17751040.md) told Netanyahu: "Every day in captivity was extremely challenging. We were in tunnels, terrified that it would not be Hamas, but Israel, that would kill us, and then they would say Hamas killed you."[^cite_note-333][^cite_note-334]&#x20;



U.S. President Joe Biden meets with [Liat Atzili](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liat_Atzili), an Israeli-American who was freed in November after being held hostage by Hamas on 8 July 2024, in the White House

In January 2024, the mother of Ron Sherman, a deceased hostage whose body was recovered by the IDF, accused the Israeli military of killing her son by filling the tunnel he was in with poison gas.[^cite_note-335] (Sherman was killed by an Israeli airstrike on 12 December.[^cite_note-jpost20240910-336]) About 20 relatives of hostages still held as captives by Hamas stormed a [Knesset](74656.md) finance committee on 22 January, a day after Netanyahu rejected new Hamas conditions for ending the war and releasing the remaining hostages. Many voiced their displeasure at the apparent lack of resolution in having the remaining hostages freed.[^cite_note-337][^cite_note-338]&#x20;

That same month, the group [Tsav 9](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tsav_9) was created on Facebook and grew to over 400 members of family members of those taken hostage. The group became active in disrupting the delivery of humanitarian aid into the Gaza Strip. The group actions have caused it to be characterized as a "violent extremist" group by the United States in June 2024.[^cite_note-339][^cite_note-340]&#x20;

After a "Settlement Brings Security" ([Hebrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hebrew_language): התיישבות מביאה ביטחון, [romanized](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romanization_of_Hebrew): *Hitiashvút Mevi'ávi Takhón*) Conference was held in [Jerusalem](16043.md) on 28 January 2024, sponsored by the right-wing [Nachala (Israeli settler organization)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nachala_\(organisation\)) pushing for [resettlement of the Gaza Strip](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proposed_Israeli_resettlement_of_the_Gaza_Strip), many family members expressed their concerns about the conference in context of the crisis. It was strongly criticized for the festive atmosphere, for the movement's forming a perceived impediment to the hostages' release, and for its calls for coerced emigration of Palestinians from their country. One family member stated of the conferencegoers "you're dancing on their blood".[^cite_note-hostage-families-341][^cite_note-jpost-342]&#x20;



[Elon Musk](909036.md) meets with President Isaac Herzog and families of hostages, 27 November 2023

A delegation of family members of Israeli hostages, along with several dozen lawyers, went to The Hague to file complaints against Hamas leaders at the International Criminal Court, which is empowered to prosecute individuals for serious violations of the Geneva Conventions that amount to war crimes.[^cite_note-343]&#x20;

After Israel's National Security Advisor [Tzachi Hanegbi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tzachi_Hanegbi) in May 2024 had a discussion with several relatives of the hostages, one relative told the media that Hanegbi had indicated that Israeli Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu would only agree to a deal regarding the hostages if, according to the relative, "it's going to be politically good for \[Netanyahu], that if he sees polls that say that the Israeli public wants to see the hostages home more than it wants the continuation of the war, then he will make a deal that will bring all the hostages home".[^cite_note-344][^cite_note-345]&#x20;

In early-September 2024, hostage families stated that executives at [Channel 14](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Channel_14_\(Israel\)) had told them a hostage deal would not happen because it would threaten Netanyahu's coalition government.[^cite_note-346] On 7 September 2024, mass demonstrations were held in support of the liberation of hostages in exchange for a cease-fire deal with Hamas.[^cite_note-500k-347] The organising groups estimated that 500,000 participated in the Tel Aviv protest, with 250,000 more at rallies around the country.[^cite_note-500k-347] This made it the largest-ever rally in Israel after the street protests organised by [Peace Now](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peace_Now) on 25 September 1982, after the [Sabra and Shatila massacre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sabra_and_Shatila_massacre), with an estimated 400,000 participants, c. 10% of Israel's population;[^cite_note-CIE-348] and the [2023 Israeli judicial reform protests](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Israeli_judicial_reform_protests).[^cite_note-twih-349]&#x20;



Israelis gather in [Hostages Square](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hostages_Square) on 9 October 2025 after announcement of the [Gaza peace plan](81215079.md)

In August 2025, after the Israeli Prime Minister's Office declared its control over Gaza City, the spouse of one hostage remarked, "This is not merely a military decision. It could represent a death sentence for those we cherish the most." The spouse of another hostage stated, "Following two years of conflict, there has been no advancement."[^cite_note-350]&#x20;

In the aftermath of the [Israeli assault on Hamas leaders in Dodah in September 2025](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Israeli_attack_on_Doha), numerous families of the hostages, along with their supporters, organized marches in Tel Aviv, Jerusalem, Haifa, Karmi Gat, Beersheba, and various other cities and towns throughout the nation. They expressed their belief that the attack was endangering diplomatic initiatives aimed at securing the release of prisoners who had been detained in Gaza for 708 days. "We are currently witnessing yet another intentional effort to undermine the hostage return agreement," stated the association in a press release. "This is not a conflict aimed at toppling Hamas, but rather a conflict that poses a threat to the stability of Israeli society – and we will not allow that to occur!" They called for an immediate cessation of hostilities.[^cite_note-351]&#x20;

### International



On 12 December 2023, the [UN General Assembly](31957.md) passed [Resolution ES-10/22](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_General_Assembly_Resolution_ES-10/22) calling for an immediate ceasefire and the "immediate and unconditional" release of the hostages.

On 16 October 2023, a report sent to the [International Committee of the Red Cross](5023832.md) by the [Geneva](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Geneva)-based organization Hostage and Missing Families Forum medical team, headed by [Hagai Levine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hagai_Levine), said hostages are "in urgent need of treatment and lifesaving medication" and are "prone to immediate mortality." The report also expressed concern about babies requiring [infant formula](65330.md).[^cite_note-352][^cite_note-353]&#x20;

&#x20;On 25 October 2023, [Qatar](8238258.md)'s Prime Minister and Foreign Minister [Mohammed bin Abdulrahman bin Jassim Al Thani](49695374.md) stated that there had been some progress in the hostage negotiations.[^cite_note-354] That same day, [World Health Organization](33583.md) Director General [Tedros Adhanom Ghebreyesus](39712205.md) met with the Hostages and Missing Families Forum and said:

> There is an urgent need for the captors of the hostages to provide signs of life, proof of provision of health care and the immediate release, on humanitarian and health grounds, of all those abducted.[^cite_note-355]

[US State department](31975.md) spokesman, [Mathew Miller](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matthew_Miller_\(spokesperson\)) suggested in response to a question about evidence of Hamas [sexual and gender based violence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_and_gender-based_violence_in_the_7_October_attack_on_Israel) on 7 October, that the reason Hamas was holding on to female Israeli hostages was to prevent them from testifying about their experiences in Hamas captivity.[^cite_note-356][^cite_note-357]&#x20;

In October 2024, a government building in [Tehran](57654.md)—frequently used for displaying murals in both Hebrew and Persian—was updated to feature a collage of photographs of hostages held by Hamas, accompanied by the phrase "No hostages will be freed," shown in both languages.[^cite_note-358]&#x20;

| 8 October 2023 | Analysis from the Washington Post determines that four hostages were killed shortly after being taken captive in Kibbutz [Be'eri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Be%27eri).[^cite_note-KellyCahlan2023-89] |
| 9 October 2023 | [Hamas](13913.md)'s [Al-Qassam Brigades](1703333.md) threatens to execute hostages if civilian homes are bombed "without warning". |
| 14 October 2023 | Hamas reports that nine hostages had been killed over a 24-hour period due to sustained IDF airstrikes. Israeli officials responded that as the hostages were in Hamas custody, the hostages well being was their responsibility.[^cite_note-independent20231014-160] |
| 17 October 2023 | Hamas releases a video of hostage [Mia Schem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mia_Schem); after her release she describes the video was forced against her will.[^cite_note-Kottasová2023-197][^cite_note-369] |
| 20 October 2023 | Hamas releases two American hostages unilaterally.[^cite_note-ABC-Hutchinson-146] |
| 23 October 2023 | Hamas releases two elderly Israeli female hostages unilaterally.[^cite_note-Guardian-Burke-32] |
| 30 October 2023 | Israeli forces [rescue Ori Megidish](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rescue_of_Ori_Megidish), an Israeli female soldier that had been held hostage in northern Gaza.[^cite_note-370] Hamas releases a video showing three female hostages pleading to be released.[^cite_note-Reals30102023-317] |
| 4 November 2023 | Hamas claims that more than 60 hostages are missing "because of Israeli airstrikes on Gaza."[^cite_note-Reuters04112023-161] |
| 9 November 2023 | Abu Obaida, spokesperson for Hamas's Al-Qassam Brigades, announced the release of two Israeli-American hostages, a mother and daughter, citing "humanitarian reasons." They were later handed over to Israeli forces at the Gaza Strip border.[^cite_note-371] |
| 14 November 2023 | Three hostages are killed by an Israeli airstrike which targeted and killed Hamas Gaza City Brigade Chief [Ahmed Ghandour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ahmed_Ghandour_\(militant\)) on 14 November.[^cite_note-jpost20240910-336][^cite_note-timesofisrael20240910-372] Their bodies were recovered by the IDF on 15 December but the Israeli government did not publicly reveal the cause of their death until September 2024.[^cite_note-jpost20240910-336][^cite_note-timesofisrael20240910-372] A Hamas statement issued 22 December 2023 along with video of the hostages had stated that the organization "tried to keep them alive – but Netanyahu insisted on killing them."[^cite_note-jpost20240910-336] [Israel](9282173.md) [Elia Toledano](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elia_Toledano), [Nik Beizer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nik_Beizer), and [Ron Sherman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ron_Sherman) |
| 16 November 2023 | Israel announces that it has recovered the bodies of two hostages, [Yehudit Weiss](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yehudit_Waiss) and Corporal [Noa Marciano](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Noa_Marciano), from buildings adjacent to [Al-Shifa hospital](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Shifa_hospital).[^cite_note-Boxerman16112023-25] |
| 17 November 2023 | Hamas releases a video confirming the death of 86-year-old hostage Arye Zalmanovich, claiming he has died due to a heart attack caused by IDF airstrikes.[^cite_note-newsweek20231118-167] |
| 21 November 2023 | Israel releases footage that it says shows hostages being taken into Al-Shifa hospital by armed men. According to Israel, a third hostage, Corporal Noa Marciano, was taken to Al-Shifa hospital and executed there. Hamas had previously claimed that Marciano was killed in an Israeli airstrike on 9 November.[^cite_note-Beake-2023-90] |
| 24–30 November 2023 | Hamas and Israel agree to a four-day ceasefire in which 50 Israeli hostages would be released for 150 [Palestinian prisoners](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Palestinian_prisoners_in_Israel). The ceasefire was extended by three additional days. The hostages to be released during the ceasefire included women and minors.[^cite_note-373][^cite_note-374] Hamas releases 80 Israeli hostages in exchange for the release of 240 prisoners during the weeklong ceasefire.[^cite_note-TOI231130-18] Separate from the broader hostage release, on 26 November 2023, Hamas releases a dual citizen Russian-Israeli hostage, "as a result of the movement's appreciation for President Putin's positions".[^cite_note-375] Hamas also released 23 Thai hostages and one Filipino hostage during the weeklong ceasefire in a separate agreement between Hamas and the Thai government.[^cite_note-Setboonsarng2023-114][^cite_note-376] [Israel](9282173.md) 81 from Israel [Thailand](30128.md) 23 from Thailand [Philippines](23440.md) 1 from the Philippines |
| 29 November 2023 | Hamas reports that members of the [Bibas family](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kidnapping_of_the_Bibas_family), a 10-month old, a 4-year-old and their mother were apparently killed by Israeli airstrikes in Gaza.[^cite_note-Talmazan_2023-165]  The IDF and Shin Bet announce they have recovered the body of deceased hostage [Ophir Sarfati](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ofir_Tzarfati) in recent days.[^cite_note-377][^cite_note-Tzarfati-378] |
| 1 December 2023 | Four hostages from [Nir Oz](17751040.md) were announced to have died in captivity.[^cite_note-379] |
| 8 December 2023 | Hamas says hostage Sahar Baruch was killed in a failed rescue attempt.[^cite_note-CNN_2023-135] On 3 January 2024, the IDF admitted that Baruch was killed during a failed rescue attempt and two Israeli soldiers were wounded while attempting the rescue attempt.[^cite_note-380] |
| 12 December 2023 | The IDF recovers the bodies of two hostages in [underground tunnels](43413500.md) in [Jabalia](453723.md), Gaza:[^cite_note-381] They were: [Israel](9282173.md) [Eden Zechariah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eden_Zecharya) and [Ziv Dado](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ziv_Dado) |
| 15 December 2023 | Three Israeli hostages are [shot dead](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Killing_of_Alon_Shamriz,_Yotam_Haim,_and_Samer_Talalka) by the IDF while waving a white-flag in [Shuja'iyya](20956585.md), Gaza in a friendly fire incident.[^cite_note-382] The IDF recovers the bodies of three additional hostages in Gaza.[^cite_note-383] [Israel](9282173.md) Alon Shamriz [Israel](9282173.md) Yotam Haim [Israel](9282173.md) Samer Talalka |
| 18 December 2023 | Hamas released a video of three elderly Israeli hostages on 18 December.[^cite_note-384] |
| 19 December 2023 | Palestinian Islamic Jihad released a video of two Israeli hostages on 19 December.[^cite_note-385] |
| 4 January 2024 | Three Israelis that had been previously presumed as missing are announced as held hostage in Gaza. Israel believes 136 hostages are being held in the Gaza Strip.[^cite_note-386][^cite_note-387] |
| 15 January 2024 | Hamas releases a video showing three Israeli hostages. Hamas claims that two of the hostages were killed in Israeli airstrikes. Israel disputed the claim.[^cite_note-388] |
| 19 January 2024 | The [Al-Nasser Salah al-Deen Brigades](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Nasser_Salah_al-Deen_Brigades) released a video of an Israeli captive who it claimed was killed by an Israeli airstrike in the Gaza Strip.[^cite_note-389][^cite_note-390] |
| 6 February 2024 | According to a confidential assessment conducted by Israeli intelligence officers, it was reported that there were more deceased hostages then previously known, with an additional 20 hostages reportedly deceased.[^cite_note-Berman_2024-176] |
| 12 February 2024 | The IDF, Shin Bet and special police forces [rescue two hostages](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Rafah_hostage_raid), who were kidnapped from [Kibbutz Nir Yitzhak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nir_Yitzhak_attack), from a building in [Rafah](584373.md) in southern Gaza, one soldier is reportedly injured and 37 Hamas militants are reportedly killed.[^cite_note-israelhayom_2024-137][^cite_note-Nidal_Al-Mughrabi_2024-138] |
| 6 April 2024 | The IDF announces it has recovered the body of deceased hostage [Elad Katzir](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elad_Katzir).[^cite_note-391] |
| 10 April 2024 | Hamas tells mediators that it does not have 40 hostages that match the criteria for a ceasefire deal.[^cite_note-392][^cite_note-cnn20240410-107] |
| 17 May 2024 | The IDF reports that they have recovered the bodies of three of the hostages, including that of [Shani Louk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shani_Louk), who were reportedly kidnapped during the attack on the Nova festival. Per the IDF, all three were killed near the site of the attack and their bodies were taken into Gaza and later recovered in a Hamas tunnel.[^cite_note-bbc20240517-139] |
| 18 May 2024 | It was announced by the IDF that they had also recovered the body of deceased hostage [Ron Benjamin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ron_Binyamin), and his body was recovered alongside those announced the day before. Benjamin was 53-years-old and abducted from the [Be'eri kibbutz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Be%27eri) while riding his bike on the day of the [attack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Be%27eri_massacre).[^cite_note-393] |
| 24 May 2024 | The IDF reports that they have recovered the bodies of three hostages from a tunnel in Jabalia.[^cite_note-394] |
| 28 May 2024 | [Saraya Al-Quds](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saraya_Al-Quds) released a video of an Israeli hostage.[^cite_note-AJA_-_Saraya_hostage_video-395] |
| 3 June 2024 | The IDF reports the deaths of four hostages, possibly by IDF fire in Khan Yunis.[^cite_note-396] |
| 8 June 2024 | Four hostages are successfully [rescued](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Nuseirat_rescue_operation) by the IDF in an operation in Nuseirat in central Gaza. The four hostages were taken during the [Nova music festival massacre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Re%27im_music_festival_massacre). One Israeli officer and dozens of Palestinians are killed in the rescue operation.[^cite_note-397] |
| 22 July 2024 | The IDF confirmed the death of Polish-Israeli historian and educator [Alex Dancyg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alex_Dancyg), and of [Yagev Buchshtav](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yagev_Buchshtab) – both abducted from their homes in [Kibbutz Nir Oz](75042438.md) and [Kibbutz Nirim](75112633.md) during the 7 October (2023) massacres. Buchshstav's wife – [Rimon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rimon_Kirsht-Buchshtab) – was kidnapped along with her husband, but she was released in November 2023 in a hostage deal with Hamas.[^cite_note-398] |
| 24 July 2024 | Five bodies of hostages are recovered by the IDF in a tunnel in Khan Younis in southern Gaza.[^cite_note-399] |

| 27 August 2024 | A [Negev Bedouin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Negev_Bedouin) hostage, [Farhan Al-Qadi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Farhan_Al-Qadi), is rescued by Israeli forces operating in a tunnel in Rafah. Al-Qadi had been left alone and unguarded in a tunnel, and IDF forces did not encounter any resistance from Hamas when they rescued him.[^cite_note-401] |
| 28 August 2024 | The remains of an IDF soldier who was killed and abducted on 7 October were recovered from the southern Gaza Strip.[^cite_note-402] |

According to an autopsy report performed by the [Abu Kabir Forensic Institute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abu_Kabir_Forensic_Institute), the hostages were [executed by Hamas from "close range" 2–3 days earlier](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Hamas_execution_of_six_hostages\&action=edit\&redlink=1).[^cite_note-403] |

| 22 February 2025 | Six Israeli men are released by Hamas, including two who had been kidnapped before the 7 October 2023 attack and held hostage for a decade:[^cite_note-415] [Israel](9282173.md) Eliya Cohen [Israel](9282173.md) [Omer Shem Tov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Omer_Shem_Tov) [Israel](9282173.md) Omer Wenkert [Israel](9282173.md) [Austria](26964606.md) [Italy](14532.md) [Tal Shoham](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tal_Shoham) [Israel](9282173.md) [Ethiopia](187749.md) [Avera Mengistu](47192748.md) [Israel](9282173.md) [Hisham al-Sayed](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hisham_al-Sayed) |
| 26 February 2025 | The bodies of four dead Israeli hostages are returned by Hamas:[^cite_note-416] [Israel](9282173.md) [Shlomo Mansour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shlomo_Mansour) [Israel](9282173.md) [France](5843419.md) [Ohad Yahalomi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ohad_Yahalomi) [Israel](9282173.md) [Tsachi Idan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tsachi_Idan) [Israel](9282173.md) [Denmark](76972.md) [Itzik Elgarat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Itzhak_Elgarat)[^cite_note-417] |
| 12 May 2025 | [Israel](9282173.md)[United States](3434750.md) Israeli-American hostage and IDF soldier [Edan Alexander](80373555.md) is handed over to the Red Cross as a gesture of goodwill prior to [US President](24113.md) [Donald Trump](4848272.md)'s visit to the [Arabian Peninsula](47858.md) and to facilitate the entrance of [humanitarian aid](76066918.md) into Gaza.[^cite_note-418] A week later, the Israeli government resumes humanitarian aid into Gaza with reports saying that the aid is in exchange for Alexander's release.[^cite_note-419] |
| 5 June 2025 | The IDF recovers the bodies of two dead hostages:[^cite_note-420] [Israel](9282173.md) [United States](3434750.md) [Gadi Haggai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gad_Haggai) [Israel](9282173.md) [United States](3434750.md) [Canada](5042916.md) [Judi Weinstein](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judi_Weinstein-Haggai) |
| 6 June 2025 | The IDF recovers the body of [Thailand](30128.md) Thai hostage [Nattapong Pinta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kidnapping_and_killing_of_Nattapong_Pinta). The announcement came the following day. In the announcement, Israel stated that it believes Nattapong was kidnapped alive and killed in captivity.[^cite_note-421] |
| 11 June 2025 | The IDF recovers the bodies of two hostages who were killed on 7 October 2023:[^cite_note-422][^cite_note-423] [Israel](9282173.md) [Aviv Atzili](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aviv_Atzili) [Israel](9282173.md) [Yair Yaakov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yair_Yaakov) |
| 21 June 2025 | The IDF recovers the bodies of three hostages who were killed on 7 October 2023, including one IDF soldier:[^cite_note-424] [Israel](9282173.md) [Ofra Keidar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ofra_Keidar) [Israel](9282173.md) Staff Sgt. [Shay Levinson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shay_Levinson) [Israel](9282173.md) [Jonathan Samerano](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yonatan_Samerano) |
| 29 August 2025 | The IDF recovers the bodies of two hostages who were killed on 7 October 2023:[^cite_note-425][^cite_note-426] [Israel](9282173.md) [Idan Shtivi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Idan_Shtivi) [Israel](9282173.md) [Hungary](13275.md) [Ilan Weiss](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ilan_Weiss) |
| 8 October 2025 | Israel and Hamas agree on the first stage of the [Gaza war peace plan](81215079.md), under which all hostages will be released - first the living, then the dead.[^cite_note-427] |

| 14 October 2025 | The bodies of three dead Israeli hostages, including one IDF soldier, are returned by Hamas into the custody of the Red Cross and returned to Israel, along with a fourth body belonging to a Palestinian from the West Bank: [Israel](9282173.md) [Germany](11867.md) Staff Sgt. [Tamir Nimrodi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tamir_Nimrodi) [Israel](9282173.md) [Uriel Baruch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uriel_Baruch) [Israel](9282173.md) [Eitan Levy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eitan_Levy) Hamas claims the fourth body belongs to an Israeli soldier allegedly killed and abducted in May 2024, but the IDF denies the claim.[^cite_note-433] The Israeli broadcaster Kan reports that it belongs to a detained Palestinian prisoner whom the IDF used as a human shield.[^cite_note-434] |

| 9 November 2025 | Hamas returns the body of IDF soldier [Israel](9282173.md) Lt. [Hadar Goldin](44119575.md), who was killed and captured in 2014, to Israel.[^cite_note-450] |
| 13 November 2025 | Hamas and the PIJ return the body of [Israel](9282173.md) Meny Godard to Israel.[^cite_note-451] |
| 25 November 2025 | Hamas and the PIJ return the body of [Israel](9282173.md) Dror Or to Israel.[^cite_note-ToI26112025-452] |
| 3 December 2025 | Hamas and the PIJ return the body of [Thailand](30128.md) [Sudthisak Rinthalak](81382917.md) to Israel.[^cite_note-453] |
| 26 January 2026 | The IDF recovers the body of [Israel](9282173.md) Master Sgt. Ran Gvili, the last Israeli hostage left in Gaza.[^cite_note-APNews260126-28] |

[^cite_note-4]: &#x20;Many Israeli citizens taken hostage had [citizenship from additional countries](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multiple_citizenship).<sup>[\[1\]](#cite_note-1)</sup><sup>[\[2\]](#cite_note-2)</sup><sup>[\[3\]](#cite_note-3)</sup>

[^cite_note-5]: &#x20;All living captives were released by 13 October 2025. The remains of the last remaining captive were recovered on 26 January 2026.

[^cite_note-6]: &#x20;Excludes four hostages held in Gaza prior to the 7 October attacks, and 17 additional hostages held inside Israel on the day of the attack.

[^cite_note-notincludingeither-21]: &#x20;Excludes [Oron Shaul and Hadar Goldin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deaths_and_ransoming_of_Oron_Shaul_and_Hadar_Goldin), whose bodies were captured prior to the war; Shaul's body was recovered during the war and Goldin's under the Gaza peace plan.

[^cite_note-364]: &#x20; Including [Samer Talalka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Killing_of_Alon_Shamriz,_Yotam_Haim,_and_Samer_Talalka).

[^cite_note-386]: &#x20;Includes four hostages taken prior to the 7 October attacks

[^cite_note-429]: &#x20;Rom Braslavski and Bar Kupershtein were also IDF soldiers, but were on leave when taken captive.<sup>[\[422\]](#cite_note-428)</sup>

[^cite_note-432]: &#x20;Herkin gained Russian citizenship during his captivity through the effort of his mother, who hoped it would help secure his release.<sup>[\[424\]](#cite_note-431)</sup>

[^cite_note-Aseer-456]: &#x20; The release of key allied leaders has been a repeated demand from Hamas during hostage negotiations. They have repeatedly requested the release of 3 key prisoners: [Marwan Barghouti](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marwan_Barghouti) from [Fatah](11104.md), [Ahmad Saadat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ahmad_Saadat) (Secretary-General of the [Popular Front for the Liberation of Palestine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Popular_Front_for_the_Liberation_of_Palestine), abbreviated PFLP), and [Abdullah Barghouti](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abdullah_Barghouti) from their own [Qassam Brigades](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qassam_Brigades).<sup>[\[446\]](#cite_note-454)</sup> Hamas have been attempting to free [Ahmad Saadat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ahmad_Saadat) since 2006, when Hamas won the election – making [Ismail Haniyeh](41207463.md) [Prime Minister of Palestine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Palestine) – and Saadat won a seat in the [Palestinian Legislative Council](40067464.md) as the lead candidates on the PFLP [party list](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Party_list). Israel's first response to this plan was to kidnap Saadat from Palestinian Authority's Jericho prison in [Operation Bringing Home the Goods](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operation_Bringing_Home_the_Goods).<sup>[\[447\]](#cite_note-455)</sup>
